{"parentUuid":"b1b5d02e-383f-4303-b767-caa53e8f4d68","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"8e912781-feb5-43c6-b835-33fcc7719991","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CkAJHQmBe7EksAr5ubcAi2","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Security checklist\n2\t\n3\tWhat must be true before you submit. *How* to make each one true is your work —\n4\tthat is the point of the tickets.\n5\t\n6\t| # | Must be true | How the grader checks it |\n7\t|---|---|---|\n8\t| 1 | Stored passwords are argon2 hashes | Reads the seeded database and looks at the stored value |\n9\t| 2 | Wrong passwords are refused, and the message does not reveal whether the account exists | Tries a bad password |\n10\t| 3 | Every state-changing form (POST) carries a CSRF token and rejects a request without one | Replays each POST with the token removed |\n11\t| 4 | An editor cannot reach any admin-only route | Logs in as the seeded editor and requests each admin route |\n12\t| 5 | An anonymous visitor is redirected to login, never shown admin content | Requests admin routes with no session |\n13\t| 6 | A deactivated user cannot log in | Deactivates the seeded editor, then tries to log in |\n14\t| 7 | User-written Markdown cannot inject scripts | Saves a post containing `<script>` and an `onerror=` attribute, publishes, and checks both the preview and the export |\n15\t| 8 | Drafts never appear in `site/` or on the public site | Publishes with a draft present and searches the output |\n16\t| 9 | No secrets, databases, or `.env` files in git history | Greps the full history |\n17\t| 10 | Access control is enforced in code, not by hiding links | Requests admin URLs directly rather than clicking |\n18\t\n19\tRule of thumb: if the only thing stopping an editor from reaching a page is that\n20\tthe link is hidden, it is not stopping anyone.\n21\t"}]},"uuid":"5a6343fe-b9fe-44af-86e8-13450e88ae9f","timestamp":"2026-09-29T03:11:43.091Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/SECURITY-CHECKLIST.md","content":"# Security checklist\n\nWhat must be true before you submit. *How* to make each one true is your work —\nthat is the point of the tickets.\n\n| # | Must be true | How the grader checks it |\n|---|---|---|\n| 1 | Stored passwords are argon2 hashes | Reads the seeded database and looks at the stored value |\n| 2 | Wrong passwords are refused, and the message does not reveal whether the account exists | Tries a bad password |\n| 3 | Every state-changing form (POST) carries a CSRF token and rejects a request without one | Replays each POST with the token removed |\n| 4 | An editor cannot reach any admin-only route | Logs in as the seeded editor and requests each admin route |\n| 5 | An anonymous visitor is redirected to login, never shown admin content | Requests admin routes with no session |\n| 6 | A deactivated user cannot log in | Deactivates the seeded editor, then tries to log in |\n| 7 | User-written Markdown cannot inject scripts | Saves a post containing `<script>` and an `onerror=` attribute, publishes, and checks both the preview and the export |\n| 8 | Drafts never appear in `site/` or on the public site | Publishes with a draft present and searches the output |\n| 9 | No secrets, databases, or `.env` files in git history | Greps the full history |\n| 10 | Access control is enforced in code, not by hiding links | Requests admin URLs directly rather than clicking |\n\nRule of thumb: if the only thing stopping an editor from reaching a page is that\nthe link is hidden, it is not stopping anyone.\n","numLines":21,"startLine":1,"totalLines":21}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b1b5d02e-383f-4303-b767-caa53e8f4d68","session_id":"a2316d11-3941-4d87-98fe-7d21dea1d9fa","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6d5bfbb1-1cff-4ca4-9c04-cac951b8d95d","version":"2.1.280","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"2e6bbb79-be81-44cb-9492-699f40b2f701","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"8e912781-feb5-43c6-b835-33fcc7719991","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019EeNRVtcCP2SEnpUWpDZe4","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n2\t\n3\tT00 publishes a placeholder home page. As you build content types, extend\n4\trender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n5\t\n6\t  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n7\t  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n8\t     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n9\t\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\timport shutil\n13\tfrom pathlib import Path\n14\t\n15\tfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n16\t\n17\tfrom app import settings\n18\t\n19\tCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n20\t:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\n21\tbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\n22\theader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\n23\tmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n24\t\"\"\"\n25\t\n26\t\n27\tdef environment() -> Environment:\n28\t    return Environment(\n29\t        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n30\t        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n31\t    )\n32\t\n33\t\n34\tdef render_site(out: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n35\t    out = out or settings.SITE\n36\t    if out.exists():\n37\t        shutil.rmtree(out)\n38\t    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n39\t    env = environment()\n40\t    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n41\t    (out / \"index.html\").write_text(\n42\t        env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(\n43\t            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, items=[], css_path=\"style.css\",\n44\t            home_path=\"index.html\",\n45\t        )\n46\t    )\n47\t    return out\n48\t"}]},"uuid":"03468c7a-f600-4e05-83d9-2b8cc27577b4","timestamp":"2026-09-29T03:11:46.258Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/publish.py","content":"\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nT00 publishes a placeholder home page. As you build content types, extend\nrender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    env = environment()\n    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n    (out / \"index.html\").write_text(\n        env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(\n            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, items=[], css_path=\"style.css\",\n            home_path=\"index.html\",\n        )\n    )\n    return out\n","numLines":48,"startLine":1,"totalLines":48}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2e6bbb79-be81-44cb-9492-699f40b2f701","session_id":"a2316d11-3941-4d87-98fe-7d21dea1d9fa","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6d5bfbb1-1cff-4ca4-9c04-cac951b8d95d","version":"2.1.280","gitBranch":"main"}

So you cannot plan in tokens. {"parentUuid":"27867327-00b5-49f9-b58d-ec306b182051","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"8e912781-feb5-43c6-b835-33fcc7719991","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01T8dgEYSwQXpSGy9AXjcnyd","type":"tool_result","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Check your submission before you send it. The judge runs this first.\n\n    uv run python scripts/check_submission.py --stage 1\n    uv run python scripts/check_submission.py --stage 2\n    uv run python scripts/check_submission.py --stage 2 --json\n\nStage 0 is the instructor's check of the template itself.\nExit code 0 means every check passed.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport re\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\nSECRET = re.compile(r\"(sk-[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}|ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN\\s*[:=]\\s*\\S{8,}|-----BEGIN [A-Z ]*PRIVATE KEY)\")\nREPORT = re.compile(r\"^iphs400_mp2-web-cms_report_[a-z]+-[a-z-]+_\\d{8}\\.md$\")\nTRANSCRIPT = re.compile(r\"^iphs400_mp2-cms_chat-session_\\d{2}_[a-z]+-[a-z-]+_\\d{8}\\.md$\")\nSCREENS = [\"login\", \"dashboard\", \"content-list\", \"editor\", \"users\", \"editor-denied\"]\n\n\ndef sh(*args: str) -> str:\n    return subprocess.run(args, cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True).stdout\n\n\nclass Checks:\n    def __init__(self) -> None:\n        self.results: list[dict] = []\n\n    def add(self, ok: bool, name: str, detail: str, fix: str = \"\") -> None:\n        self.results.append({\"check\": name, \"ok\": bool(ok), \"detail\": detail, \"fix\": fix})\n\n    # --- checks -----------------------------------------------------------\n    def hygiene(self) -> None:\n        tracked = sh(\"git\", \"ls-files\").splitlines()\n        bad = [f for f in tracked if f.endswith((\".db\", \".env\")) or f.startswith(\".claude/state/\")]\n        self.add(not bad, \"no database or .env committed\", \", \".join(bad) or \"clean\",\n                 \"git rm --cached <file>, confirm .gitignore covers it, then rewrite history if it is old\")\n        history = sh(\"git\", \"log\", \"--all\", \"-p\", \"--\", \".\")\n        hits = sorted(set(SECRET.findall(history)))\n        self.add(not hits, \"no secrets in history\", f\"{len(hits)} match(es)\",\n                 \"rotate the key first, then ask Claude to rewrite history\")\n        self.add((ROOT / \".env.example\").exists(), \".env.example present\", \"\", \"restore it from the template\")\n\n    def docs(self, stage: int) -> None:\n        pairs = [\n            (\"notes/cms-field-notes.md\", \"field notes\"),\n            (\"CONTEXT.md\", \"glossary\"),\n            (\"notes/token-budget-plan.md\", \"token budget plan\"),\n            (\"notes/usage-ledger.csv\", \"usage ledger\"),\n            (\"docs/process/compaction-log.md\", \"compaction log\"),\n        ]\n        if stage >= 2:\n            pairs += [(\"README.md\", \"README\"), (\"scripts/seed_demo.py\", \"seed script\")]\n        for path, label in pairs:\n            self.add((ROOT / path).exists(), f\"{label} present\", path, f\"create {path}\")\n        if stage >= 2:\n            handoffs = list((ROOT / \"docs\" / \"handoff\").glob(\"*.md\"))\n            self.add(bool(handoffs), \"at least one handoff saved\", f\"{len(handoffs)} file(s)\",\n                     \"copy a /handoff document into docs/handoff/\")\n\n    def naming(self, stage: int) -> None:\n        reports = list((ROOT / \"docs\").glob(\"iphs400_mp2-web-cms_report_*.md\"))\n        if stage >= 2:\n            ok = any(REPORT.match(p.name) for p in reports)\n            self.add(ok, \"report named correctly\",\n                     \", \".join(p.name for p in reports) or \"missing\",\n                     \"iphs400_mp2-web-cms_report_{first}-{last}_{YYYYMMDD}.md, lowercase\")\n        transcripts = list((ROOT / \"docs\" / \"transcripts\").glob(\"*.md\"))\n        bad = [p.name for p in transcripts if not TRANSCRIPT.match(p.name)]\n        self.add(transcripts and not bad, \"transcripts present and named correctly\",\n                 f\"{len(transcripts)} file(s); bad: {bad or 'none'}\",\n                 \"iphs400_mp2-cms_chat-session_{NN}_{first}-{last}_{YYYYMMDD}.md\")\n\n    def readme(self, stage: int) -> None:","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"4580c6bb-f10c-46dd-adff-05c42ad07e82","timestamp":"2026-09-29T03:13:33.435Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Check your submission before {"parentUuid":"24e0fef1-142b-49a6-ae24-bacbcb4ec38a","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5","id":"msg_011CfX12WUYqfTuCvJeiBJdF","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Q1sa7QPEJTYAouhnp78YTQ","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/tests/test_login.py","content":"\"\"\"T01: login and sessions.\n\nCovers login success (both seeded roles), a wrong password, logout-then-\nblocked, and a deactivated user being refused — plus the CSRF and password\nstorage protections the security checklist requires now, not deferred to the\naudit ticket.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom tests.conftest import DEMO_USERS\n\n\ndef _csrf_login_page(client):\n    response = client.get(\"/login\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    return response.cookies[\"csrf_token\"]\n\n\ndef test_login_success_reaches_themed_landing(client):\n    for role, user in DEMO_USERS.items():\n        csrf = _csrf_login_page(client)\n        response = client.post(\n            \"/login\",\n            data={\"email\": user[\"email\"], \"password\": user[\"password\"], \"csrf_token\": csrf},\n            follow_redirects=False,\n        )\n        assert response.status_code == 303\n        dashboard = client.get(response.headers[\"location\"])\n        assert dashboard.status_code == 200\n        assert role in dashboard.text.lower() or user[\"email\"] in dashboard.text\n        client.post(\"/logout\")\n\n\ndef test_wrong_password_is_refused_with_no_session(client):\n    csrf = _csrf_login_page(client)\n    response = client.post(\n        \"/login\",\n        data={\n            \"email\": DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"email\"],\n            \"password\": \"not-the-password\",\n            \"csrf_token\": csrf,\n        },\n        follow_redirects=False,\n    )\n    assert response.status_code == 401\n    assert \"session\" not in client.cookies\n    assert \"invalid\" in response.text.lower()\n\n\ndef test_logout_then_admin_route_is_blocked(client):\n    csrf = _csrf_login_page(client)\n    login = client.post(\n        \"/login\",\n        data={\n            \"email\": DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"email\"],\n            \"password\": DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"password\"],\n            \"csrf_token\": csrf,\n        },\n        follow_redirects=False,\n    )\n    assert login.status_code == 303\n\n    dashboard = client.get(\"/admin/dashboard\")\n    assert dashboard.status_code == 200\n\n    logout_csrf = client.cookies[\"csrf_token\"]\n    logout = client.post(\"/logout\", data={\"csrf_token\": logout_csrf}, follow_redirects=False)\n    assert logout.status_code == 303\n\n    blocked = client.get(\"/admin/dashboard\", follow_redirects=False)\n    assert blocked.status_code == 303\n    assert blocked.headers[\"location\"].endswith(\"/login\")\n\n\ndef test_deactivated_user_login_is_refused(client, deactivate_user):\n    deactivate_user(DEMO_USERS[\"editor\"][\"email\"])\n    csrf = _csrf_login_page(client)\n    response = client.post(\n        \"/login\",\n        data={\n            \"email\": DEMO_USERS[\"editor\"][\"email\"],\n            \"password\": DEMO_USERS[\"editor\"][\"password\"],\n            \"csrf_token\": csrf,\n        },\n        follow_redirects=False,\n    )\n    assert response.status_code == 401\n    assert \"session\" not in client.cookies\n\n\ndef test_login_without_csrf_token_is_rejected(client):\n    _csrf_login_page(client)\n    response = client.post(\n        \"/login\",\n        data={\n            \"email\": DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"email\"],\n            \"password\": DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"password\"],\n        },\n        follow_redirects=False,\n    )\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n    assert \"session\" not in client.cookies\n\n\ndef test_passwords_are_stored_as_argon2_hashes_only():\n    from app import db\n\n    conn = db.get_connection()\n    try:\n        row = conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT password_hash FROM users WHERE email = ?\",\n            (DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"email\"],),\n        ).fetchone()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n    assert row is not None\n    assert row[\"password_hash\"] != DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"password\"]\n    assert row[\"password_hash\"].startswith(\"$argon2\")\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":12645,"cache_read_input_tokens":77145,"output_tokens":1485,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":12645,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":1485,"cache_read_input_tokens":77145,"cache_creation_input_tokens":12645,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":12645},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_01Q1sa7QPEJTYAouhnp78YTQ":{"file_path":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/tests/test_login.py","content":"\"\"\"T01: seeded roles), a wrong password, logout-then-\nblocked, and a deactivated user being refused — plus the CSRF and password\nstorage protections the security checklist requires now, not deferred to the\naudit ticket.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom tests.conftest import DEMO_USERS\n\n\ndef _csrf_login_page(client):\n    response = client.get(\"/login\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    return response.cookies[\"csrf_token\"]\n\n\ndef test_login_success_reaches_themed_landing(client):\n    for role, user in DEMO_USERS.items():\n        csrf = _csrf_login_page(client)\n        response = client.post(\n            \"/login\",\n            data={\"email\": user[\"email\"], \"password\": user[\"password\"], \"csrf_token\": csrf},\n            follow_redirects=False,\n        )\n        assert response.status_code == 303\n        dashboard = client.get(response.headers[\"location\"])\n        assert dashboard.status_code == 200\n        assert role in dashboard.text.lower() or user[\"email\"] in dashboard.text\n        client.post(\"/logout\")\n\n\ndef test_wrong_password_is_refused_with_no_session(client):\n    csrf = _csrf_login_page(client)\n    response = client.post(\n        \"/login\",\n        data={\n            \"email\": DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"email\"],\n            \"password\": \"not-the-password\",\n            \"csrf_token\": csrf,\n        },\n        follow_redirects=False,\n    )\n    assert response.status_code == 401\n    assert \"session\" not in client.cookies\n    assert \"invalid\" in response.text.lower()\n\n\ndef test_logout_then_admin_route_is_blocked(client):\n    csrf = _csrf_login_page(client)\n    login = client.post(\n        \"/login\",\n        data={\n            \"email\": DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"email\"],\n            \"password\": DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"password\"],\n            \"csrf_token\": csrf,\n        },\n        follow_redirects=False,\n    )\n    assert login.status_code == 303\n\n    dashboard = client.get(\"/admin/dashboard\")\n    assert dashboard.status_code == 200\n\n    logout_csrf = client.cookies[\"csrf_token\"]\n    logout = client.post(\"/logout\", data={\"csrf_token\": logout_csrf}, follow_redirects=False)\n    assert logout.status_code == 303\n\n    blocked = client.get(\"/admin/dashboard\", follow_redirects=False)\n    assert blocked.status_code == 303\n    assert blocked.headers[\"location\"].endswith(\"/login\")\n\n\ndef test_deactivated_user_login_is_refused(client, deactivate_user):\n    deactivate_user(DEMO_USERS[\"editor\"][\"email\"])\n    csrf = _csrf_login_page(client)\n    response = client.post(\n        \"/login\",\n        data={\n            \"email\": DEMO_USERS[\"editor\"][\"email\"],\n            \"password\": DEMO_USERS[\"editor\"][\"password\"],\n            \"csrf_token\": csrf,\n        },\n        follow_redirects=False,\n    )\n    assert response.status_code == 401\n    assert \"session\" not in client.cookies\n\n\ndef test_login_without_csrf_token_is_rejected(client):\n    _csrf_login_page(client)\n    response = client.post(\n        \"/login\",\n        data={\n            \"email\": DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"email\"],\n            \"password\": DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"password\"],\n        },\n        follow_redirects=False,\n    )\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n    assert \"session\" not in client.cookies\n\n\ndef test_passwords_are_stored_as_argon2_hashes_only():\n    from app import db\n\n    conn = db.get_connection()\n    try:\n        row = conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT password_hash FROM users WHERE email = ?\",\n            (DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"email\"],),\n        ).fetchone()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n    assert row is not None\n    assert row[\"password_hash\"] != DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"password\"]\n    assert Nothing about the session lives server-side, so there is\nno session table to clean up.\n\nCSRF: the double-submit cookie pattern. `new_csrf_token` + `set_csrf_cookie`\nput a random value in a cookie *and* a hidden form field; `csrf_is_valid`\nchecks the two match. An attacker forging a cross-site POST cannot read the\ncookie, so cannot supply a matching field.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport secrets\n\nfrom argon2 import PasswordHasher\nfrom argon2.exceptions import InvalidHash, VerificationError, VerifyMismatchError\nfrom itsdangerous import BadSignature, SignatureExpired, URLSafeTimedSerializer\nfrom starlette.requests import Request\nfrom starlette.responses import Response\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nSESSION_COOKIE = \"session\"\nSESSION_MAX_AGE = 60 * 60 * 8  # 8 hours\nCSRF_COOKIE = \"csrf_token\"\n\n_hasher = PasswordHasher()\n_serializer = URLSafeTimedSerializer(settings.SECRET_KEY, salt=\"session\")\n\n\ndef hash_password(password: str) -> str:\n    return _hasher.hash(password)\n\n\ndef verify_password(password: str, password_hash: str) -> bool:\n    try:\n        return _hasher.verify(password_hash, password)\n    except (VerifyMismatchError, VerificationError, InvalidHash):\n        return False\n\n\ndef set_session_cookie(response: Response, user_id: int) -> None:\n    token = _serializer.dumps({\"user_id\": user_id})\n    response.set_cookie(\n        SESSION_COOKIE, token, max_age=SESSION_MAX_AGE,\n        httponly=True, samesite=\"lax\",\n    )\n\n\ndef clear_session_cookie(response: Response) -> None:\n    response.delete_cookie(SESSION_COOKIE)\n\n\ndef read_session(request: Request) -> int | None:\n    token = request.cookies.get(SESSION_COOKIE)\n    if not token:\n        return None\n    try:\n        data = _serializer.loads(token, max_age=SESSION_MAX_AGE)\n    except (BadSignature, SignatureExpired):\n        return None\n    return data.get(\"user_id\")\n\n\ndef new_csrf_token() -> str:\n    return secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n\n\ndef set_csrf_cookie(response: Response, token: str) -> None:\n    response.set_cookie(CSRF_COOKIE, token, httponly=True, samesite=\"lax\")\n\n\ndef csrf_is_valid(request: Request, submitted: str | None) -> bool:\n    cookie_value = request.cookies.get(CSRF_COOKIE)\n    if not cookie_value or not submitted:\n        return False\n    return secrets.compare_digest(cookie_value, {"parentUuid":"b0cb5e62-62ba-4ee3-a99c-c9188e270d2e","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5","id":"msg_011CfX17BUwVRmraGzqZFVhP","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_011kcMBTdBQs6bCvcVGkPMxA","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/routes/auth.py","content":"\"\"\"Login, logout, and the one protected landing route T01 needs.\n\nLater tickets add real admin routes; they should follow the same pattern —\n`auth.read_session` to find the user, redirect to /login if there isn't one.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Form, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import auth, db, settings\n\nrouter = APIRouter()\ntemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\n\n\ndef _current_user(request: Request) -> db.sqlite3.Row | None:\n    user_id = auth.read_session(request)\n    if user_id is None:\n        return None\n    user = db.get_user_by_id(user_id)\n    if user is None or not user[\"active\"]:\n        return None\n    return user\n\n\n@router.get(\"/login\")\ndef login_form(request: Request):\n    if _current_user(request) is not None:\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/dashboard\", status_code=303)\n    token = auth.new_csrf_token()\n    response = templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/login.html\", {\"title\": \"Log in\", \"csrf_token\": token, \"error\": None}\n    )\n    auth.set_csrf_cookie(response, token)\n    return response\n\n\n@router.post(\"/login\")\ndef login_submit(\n    request: Request,\n    email: str = Form(...),\n    password: str = Form(...),\n    csrf_token: str | None = Form(None),\n):\n    if not auth.csrf_is_valid(request, csrf_token):\n        return _rejected(request, \"Your session expired. Please try again.\", status_code=403)\n\n    user = db.get_user_by_email(email)\n    if user is None or not user[\"active\"] or not auth.verify_password(password, user[\"password_hash\"]):\n        return _rejected(request, \"Invalid email or password.\", status_code=401)\n\n    response = RedirectResponse(\"/admin/dashboard\", status_code=303)\n    auth.set_session_cookie(response, user[\"id\"])\n    return response\n\n\ndef _rejected(request: Request, message: str, *, status_code: int):\n    token = auth.new_csrf_token()\n    response = templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request,\n        \"admin/login.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Log in\", \"csrf_token\": token, \"error\": message},\n        status_code=status_code,\n    )\n    auth.set_csrf_cookie(response, token)\n    return response\n\n\n@router.post(\"/logout\")\ndef logout(request: Request, csrf_token: str | None = Form(None)):\n    if not auth.csrf_is_valid(request, csrf_token):\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=403)\n    response = RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n    auth.clear_session_cookie(response)\n    return response\n\n\n@router.get(\"/admin/dashboard\")\ndef admin_dashboard(request: Request):\n    user = _current_user(request)\n    if user is None:\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n    role_label = \"Manager\" if user[\"role\"] == \"admin\" else \"Editor\"\n    token = auth.new_csrf_token()\n    response = templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request,\n        \"admin/dashboard.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Dashboard\", \"role_label\": role_label, \"email\": user[\"email\"], \"csrf_token\": token},\n    )\n    auth.set_csrf_cookie(response, token)\n    return response\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":762,"cache_read_input_tokens":97818,"output_tokens":1550,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":207},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":762,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":1550,"cache_read_input_tokens":97818,"cache_creation_input_tokens":762,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":762},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_011kcMBTdBQs6bCvcVGkPMxA":{"file_path":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/routes/auth.py","content":"\"\"\"Login, they should follow the same pattern —\n`auth.read_session` to find the user, redirect to /login if there isn't one.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Form, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import auth, db, settings\n\nrouter = APIRouter()\ntemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\n\n\ndef _current_user(request: Request) -> db.sqlite3.Row | None:\n    user_id = auth.read_session(request)\n    if user_id is None:\n        return None\n    user = db.get_user_by_id(user_id)\n    if user is None or not user[\"active\"]:\n        return None\n    return user\n\n\n@router.get(\"/login\")\ndef login_form(request: Request):\n    if _current_user(request) is not None:\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/dashboard\", status_code=303)\n    token = auth.new_csrf_token()\n    response = templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/login.html\", {\"title\": \"Log in\", \"csrf_token\": token, \"error\": None}\n    )\n    auth.set_csrf_cookie(response, token)\n    return response\n\n\n@router.post(\"/login\")\ndef login_submit(\n    request: Request,\n    email: str = Form(...),\n    password: str = Form(...),\n    csrf_token: str | None = Form(None),\n):\n    if not auth.csrf_is_valid(request, csrf_token):\n        return _rejected(request, \"Your session expired. Please try again.\", status_code=403)\n\n    user = db.get_user_by_email(email)\n    if user is None or not user[\"active\"] or not auth.verify_password(password, user[\"password_hash\"]):\n        return _rejected(request, \"Invalid email or password.\", status_code=401)\n\n    response = RedirectResponse(\"/admin/dashboard\", status_code=303)\n    auth.set_session_cookie(response, user[\"id\"])\n    return response\n\n\ndef _rejected(request: Request, message: str, *, status_code: int):\n    token = auth.new_csrf_token()\n    response = templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request,\n        \"admin/login.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Log in\", \"csrf_token\": token, \"error\": message},\n        status_code=status_code,\n    )\n    auth.set_csrf_cookie(response, token)\n    return response\n\n\n@router.post(\"/logout\")\ndef logout(request: Request, csrf_token: str | None = Form(None)):\n    if not auth.csrf_is_valid(request, csrf_token):\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=403)\n    response = RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n    auth.clear_session_cookie(response)\n    return response\n\n\n@router.get(\"/admin/dashboard\")\ndef admin_dashboard(request: Request):\n    user = _current_user(request)\n    if user is None:\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", TODO (later tickets): seed News drafts/published items and Pages.\n    return 0","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Create demo data so a grader (and you) can use the CMS immediately.\n\n    uv run python scripts/seed_demo.py\n\nT00 has nothing to seed. As you build content types, extend this so it creates:\n  - one admin and one editor (passwords read from .env, never hard-coded)\n  - a few posts and pages, at least one draft and one published\n\nThe rubric expects this to run clean on a fresh clone with .env.example values\n(item E4), because the database itself is never committed.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nimport sys\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    admin_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD\")\n    editor_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD\")\n    if not admin_pw or not editor_pw:\n        print(\"Set CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD and CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD in .env \"\n              \"(copy .env.example).\")\n        return 1\n\n    # TODO (your tickets): create the users, then the demo content.\n    print(\"Nothing to seed yet: no content types exist. \"\n          \"Extend scripts/seed_demo.py as you build T01+.\")\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":14,"oldLines":8,"newStart":14,"newLines":16,"lines":[" "," import os"," import sys","+from pathlib import Path"," ","+sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))"," ","+from app import db  # {"parentUuid":"5e6ecc22-5db4-4396-a577-de2f7232e066","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5","id":"msg_011CfX1CkxiYQDcYYdansEj5","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"**To try it locally:**\n\n```bash\nsource .venv/bin/activate\nuv run python scripts/seed_demo.py   # creates the Demo Manager / Demo Editor accounts\nuv run cms serve                     # starts the admin console at http://localhost:8000\n```\n\nThen open `http://localhost:8000/login` in a browser:\n- **Demo Manager:** `manager@laoncemil.test` / whatever you set as `CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD` in `.env`\n- **Demo Editor:** `editor@laoncemil.test` / whatever you set as `CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD` in `.env`\n\nLogging in redirects to `/admin/dashboard` (\"Welcome, Manager\"/\"Welcome, Editor\"), and the \"Log out\" button there ends the session — after that, `/admin/dashboard` redirects back to `/login`. A wrong password shows \"Invalid email or password\" with no session created. `/admin` itself (the T00 \"hello admin\" page) stays unprotected on purpose — it's the walking-skeleton front door, not real admin content.\n\nI'll let you know what the code review turns up before committing."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":234,"cache_read_input_tokens":107723,"output_tokens":402,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":17},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":234,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":402,"cache_read_input_tokens":107723,"cache_creation_input_tokens":234,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":234},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"serverClassifierRequest":"94eb9c14-9710-4f15-8c8e-a92d73b38214","apiBlockIndex":1,"requestId":"req_011CfX1CkXS1bmk1rdmnXJ1S","attributionSkill":"mattpocock-skills:implement","attributionPlugin":"mattpocock-skills","type":"assistant","uuid":"866b8907-c04a-4f0f-bbb6-9aac21470fba","timestamp":"2026-09-29T03:15:58.146Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":null,"session_id":"a2316d11-3941-4d87-98fe-7d21dea1d9fa","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6d5bfbb1-1cff-4ca4-9c04-cac951b8d95d","version":"2.1.280","gitBranch":"main"}

Reproduced directly: `python -c \\\"import app.main\\\"` and `pytest tests/` both leave a stray cms.db at the repo root even though tests use tmp_path DBs. In a read-only CI/grading sandbox (or a repo checkout with a read-only working tree) this import-time write would raise an OSError and fail collection before any test runs.\"\n  },\n  {\n    \"file\": \"app/routes/auth.py\",\n    \"line\": 32,\n    \"summary\": \"The CSRF-token-generate + render-template + set-cookie sequence is duplicated verbatim in three places (login_form, _rejected, admin_dashboard).\",\n    \"failure_scenario\": \"Lines 32-36, 60-67, and 86-92 each independently call auth.new_csrf_token(), build a TemplateResponse, and call auth.set_csrf_cookie(response, token). A future change to CSRF cookie attributes (e.g. adding `secure=True` for deploy) requires editing three call sites; missing one silently reintroduces an unprotected form. Extracting a single helper (e.g. `_render_with_csrf(name, context, status_code=200)`) removes the duplication and the risk of drift.\"\n  }\n]\n```</result>\n<usage><subagent_tokens>67944</subagent_tokens><tool_uses>21</tool_uses><duration_ms>199584</duration_ms></usage>\n</task-notification>"}

{"parentUuid":"c658d2ff-6d88-4f36-8ba7-cec08eb34e58","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"8e8070c4-24fa-4a79-9035-b289571bd93a","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VbXcvmzdxSUmNCg6CGYXcU","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Login, logout, and the one protected landing route T01 needs.\n2\t\n3\tLater tickets add real admin routes; they should follow the same pattern —\n4\t`auth.read_session` to find the user, redirect to /login if there isn't one.\n5\t\"\"\"\n6\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n7\t\n8\tfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Form, Request\n9\tfrom fastapi.responses import PlainTextResponse, RedirectResponse\n10\tfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n11\t\n12\tfrom app import auth, db, settings\n13\t\n14\trouter = APIRouter()\n15\ttemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\n16\t\n17\t\n18\tdef _current_user(request: Request) -> db.sqlite3.Row | None:\n19\t    user_id = auth.read_session(request)\n20\t    if user_id is None:\n21\t        return None\n22\t    user = db.get_user_by_id(user_id)\n23\t    if user is None or not user[\"active\"]:\n24\t        return None\n25\t    return user\n26\t\n27\t\n28\t@router.get(\"/login\")\n29\tdef login_form(request: Request):\n30\t    if _current_user(request) is not None:\n31\t        return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/dashboard\", status_code=303)\n32\t    token = auth.new_csrf_token()\n33\t    response = templates.TemplateResponse(\n34\t        request, \"admin/login.html\", {\"title\": \"Log in\", \"csrf_token\": token, \"error\": None}\n35\t    )\n36\t    auth.set_csrf_cookie(response, token)\n37\t    return response\n38\t\n39\t\n40\t@router.post(\"/login\")\n41\tdef login_submit(\n42\t    request: Request,\n43\t    email: str = Form(...),\n44\t    password: str = Form(...),\n45\t    csrf_token: str | None = Form(None),\n46\t):\n47\t    if not auth.csrf_is_valid(request, csrf_token):\n48\t        return _rejected(request, \"Your session expired. Please try again.\", status_code=403)\n49\t\n50\t    user = db.get_user_by_email(email)\n51\t    if user is None or not user[\"active\"] or not auth.verify_password(password, user[\"password_hash\"]):\n52\t        return _rejected(request, \"Invalid email or password.\", status_code=401)\n53\t\n54\t    response = RedirectResponse(\"/admin/dashboard\", logout, and the one protected landing route T01 needs.\n\nLater tickets add real admin routes; they should follow the same pattern —\n`auth.read_session` to find the user, redirect to /login if there isn't one.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Form, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import PlainTextResponse, RedirectResponse\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import auth, db, settings\n\nrouter = APIRouter()\ntemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\n\n\ndef _current_user(request: Request) -> db.sqlite3.Row | None:\n    user_id = auth.read_session(request)\n    if user_id is None:\n        return None\n    user = db.get_user_by_id(user_id)\n    if user is None or not user[\"active\"]:\n        return None\n    return user\n\n\n@router.get(\"/login\")\ndef login_form(request: Request):\n    if _current_user(request) is not None:\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/dashboard\", status_code=303)\n    token = auth.new_csrf_token()\n    response = templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/login.html\", {\"title\": \"Log in\", \"csrf_token\": token, \"error\": None}\n    )\n    auth.set_csrf_cookie(response, token)\n    return response\n\n\n@router.post(\"/login\")\ndef login_submit(\n    request: Request,\n    email: str = Form(...),\n    password: str = Form(...),\n    csrf_token: str | None = Form(None),\n):\n    if not auth.csrf_is_valid(request, csrf_token):\n        return _rejected(request, \"Your session expired. Please try again.\", status_code=403)\n\n    user = db.get_user_by_email(email)\n    if user is None or not user[\"active\"] or not auth.verify_password(password, user[\"password_hash\"]):\n        return _rejected(request, \"Invalid email or password.\", status_code=401)\n\n    response = RedirectResponse(\"/admin/dashboard\", status_code=303)\n    auth.set_session_cookie(response, user[\"id\"])\n    return response\n\n\ndef _rejected(request: Request, message: str, *, status_code: int):\n    token = auth.new_csrf_token()\n    response = templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request,\n        \"admin/login.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Log in\", \"csrf_token\": token, \"error\": message},\n        status_code=status_code,\n    )\n    auth.set_csrf_cookie(response, token)\n    return response\n\n\n@router.post(\"/logout\")\ndef logout(request: Request, csrf_token: str | None = Form(None)):\n    if not auth.csrf_is_valid(request, csrf_token):\n        return PlainTextResponse(\"Invalid CSRF token.\", status_code=403)\n    response = RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n    auth.clear_session_cookie(response)\n    return response\n\n\n@router.get(\"/admin/dashboard\")\ndef admin_dashboard(request: Request):\n    user = _current_user(request)\n    if user is None:\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n    role_label = \"Manager\" if user[\"role\"] == \"admin\" else \"Editor\"\n    token = auth.new_csrf_token()\n    response = templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request,\n        \"admin/dashboard.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Dashboard\", \"role_label\": role_label, \"email\": user[\"email\"], \"csrf_token\": token},\n    )\n    auth.set_csrf_cookie(response, token)\n    return response\n","numLines":94,"startLine":1,"totalLines":94}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c658d2ff-6d88-4f36-8ba7-cec08eb34e58","session_id":"a2316d11-3941-4d87-98fe-7d21dea1d9fa","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6d5bfbb1-1cff-4ca4-9c04-cac951b8d95d","version":"2.1.280","gitBranch":"main"}

they should follow the same pattern —\n`auth.read_session` to find the user, redirect to /login if there isn't one.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Form, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import PlainTextResponse, RedirectResponse\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import auth, db, settings\n\nrouter = APIRouter()\ntemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\n\n\ndef _current_user(request: Request) -> db.sqlite3.Row | None:\n    user_id = auth.read_session(request)\n    if user_id is None:\n        return None\n    user = db.get_user_by_id(user_id)\n    if user is None or not user[\"active\"]:\n        return None\n    return user\n\n\ndef _render_with_csrf(request: Request, template_name: str, context: dict, *, status_code: int = 200):\n    token = auth.new_csrf_token()\n    response = templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, template_name, {**context, \"csrf_token\": token}, status_code=status_code\n    )\n    auth.set_csrf_cookie(response, token)\n    return response\n\n\n@router.get(\"/login\")\ndef login_form(request: Request):\n    if _current_user(request) is not None:\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/dashboard\", status_code=303)\n    return _render_with_csrf(request, \"admin/login.html\", {\"title\": \"Log in\", \"error\": None})\n\n\n@router.post(\"/login\")\ndef login_submit(\n    request: Request,\n    email: str = Form(...),\n    password: str = Form(...),\n    csrf_token: str | None = Form(None),\n):\n    if not auth.csrf_is_valid(request, csrf_token):\n        return _rejected(request, \"Your session expired. Please try again.\", status_code=403)\n\n    user = db.get_user_by_email(email)\n    if user is None or not user[\"active\"] or not auth.verify_password(password, user[\"password_hash\"]):\n        return _rejected(request, \"Invalid email or password.\", status_code=401)\n\n    response = RedirectResponse(\"/admin/dashboard\", status_code=303)\n    auth.set_session_cookie(response, user[\"id\"])\n    return response\n\n\ndef _rejected(request: Request, message: str, *, status_code: int):\n    token = auth.new_csrf_token()\n    response = templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request,\n        \"admin/login.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Log in\", \"csrf_token\": token, \"error\": message},\n        status_code=status_code,\n    )\n    auth.set_csrf_cookie(response, token)\n    return response\n\n\n@router.post(\"/logout\")\ndef logout(request: Request, csrf_token: str | None = Form(None)):\n    if not auth.csrf_is_valid(request, csrf_token):\n        return PlainTextResponse(\"Invalid CSRF token.\", status_code=403)\n    response = RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n    auth.clear_session_cookie(response)\n    return response\n\n\n@router.get(\"/admin/dashboard\")\ndef admin_dashboard(request: Request):\n    user = _current_user(request)\n    if user is None:\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n    role_label = \"Manager\" if user[\"role\"] == \"admin\" else \"Editor\"\n    token = auth.new_csrf_token()\n    response = templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request,\n        \"admin/dashboard.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Dashboard\", \"role_label\": role_label, \"email\": user[\"email\"], \"csrf_token\": token},\n    )\n    auth.set_csrf_cookie(response, token)\n    return response\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":61,"oldLines":15,"newStart":61,"newLines":9,"lines":[" "," "," def _rejected(request: Request, message: str, *, status_code: int):","-    token = message},","-        status_code=status_code,","+    return _render_with_csrf(","+        request, \"admin/login.html\", {\"title\": \"Log in\", \"error\": message}, status_code=status_code"," Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import db, settings\n\ntemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\n\n\ndef create_app() -> FastAPI:\n    db.init_db()\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\")\n\n    @app.get(\"/admin\")\n    def admin_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"admin/hello.html\", {\"title\": \"Admin\"}\n        )\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    from app.routes import auth\n    app.include_router(auth.router)\n\n    # Your ticket work plugs in here, e.g.\n    #   from app.routes import posts\n    #   app.include_router(posts.router)\n    return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":12,"oldLines":13,"newStart":12,"newLines":12,"lines":[" from fastapi import FastAPI, Request"," from fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates"," ","-from app import db, settings","+from app import settings"," "," templates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))"," "," "," def create_app() -> FastAPI:","-    db.init_db()","     app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\")"," ","     @app.get(\"/admin\")"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9380c303-993d-4790-a898-8baf304bcb2c","session_id":"a2316d11-3941-4d87-98fe-7d21dea1d9fa","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6d5bfbb1-1cff-4ca4-9c04-cac951b8d95d","version":"2.1.280","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"80c8a815-5bee-4a5f-8c60-f549c631e6c6","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"8e8070c4-24fa-4a79-9035-b289571bd93a","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01DMAa4KEYXJfRgnSLUsvc9E","type":"tool_result","content":"The file /Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/cli.py has been updated successfully. (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"uuid":"8bc5070e-08e7-49f6-a81e-ef6dd28074cb","timestamp":"2026-09-29T03:19:43.836Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/cli.py","oldString":"from app import settings\nfrom app.publish import render_site","newString":"from app import db, settings\nfrom app.publish import render_site","originalFile":"\"\"\"The `cms` command: serve, publish, deploy.\n\n    uv run cms serve      # admin console + public preview at http://localhost:8000\n    uv run cms publish    # render site/ from published content\n    uv run cms deploy     # push site/ to the gh-pages branch (Pages serves it)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.publish import render_site\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"cms\")\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\"command\", required=True)\n    serve = sub.add_parser(\"serve\", help=\"run the admin console locally\")\n    serve.add_argument(\"--port\", type=int, default=8000)\n    serve.add_argument(\"--reload\", action=\"store_true\", default=True)\n    sub.add_parser(\"publish\", help=\"render site/ from published content\")\n    deploy = sub.add_parser(\"deploy\", help=\"push site/ to gh-pages\")\n    deploy.add_argument(\"--message\", default=\"Publish site\")\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n\n    if args.command == \"serve\":\n        import uvicorn\n\n        uvicorn.run(\"app.main:app\", port=args.port, reload=args.reload)\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"publish\":\n        out = render_site()\n        print(f\"Wrote {out}. Preview it with:  python3 -m http.server -d {out} 8001\")\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"deploy\":\n        if not settings.SITE.exists():\n            print(\"site/ does not exist yet — run `uv run cms publish` first.\")\n            return 1\n        result = subprocess.run(\n            [sys.executable, \"-m\", \"ghp_import\", \"-n\", \"-p\", \"-m\", args.message,\n             str(settings.SITE)],\n        )\n        if result.returncode == 0:\n            print(\"Pushed to gh-pages. Settings -> Pages -> Deploy from a branch -> \"\n                  \"gh-pages / root, then wait up to 10 minutes.\")\n        return result.returncode\n    return 1\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":10,"oldLines":7,"newStart":10,"newLines":7,"lines":[" import subprocess"," import sys"," ","-from app import settings","+from app import db, settings"," from app.publish import render_site"," "," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"80c8a815-5bee-4a5f-8c60-f549c631e6c6","session_id":"a2316d11-3941-4d87-98fe-7d21dea1d9fa","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6d5bfbb1-1cff-4ca4-9c04-cac951b8d95d","version":"2.1.280","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"2a4ce71c-a3b1-4d4d-aa50-0dafcb2c2ae1","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5","id":"msg_011CfX1X1MuYUj9CsNaWiUCF","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0197EtxVJiMYvRdRLZBzmF19","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"gh issue comment 2 --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## /code-review findings (medium effort)\n\n1. **`app/main.py`: `db.init_db()` called unconditionally inside `create_app()`, and `app = create_app()` runs at module import time** — merely importing `app.main` wrote a real `cms.db` to the production `DATABASE_PATH` as a side effect (reproducible with `python -c \"import app.main\"`). In a read-only checkout this would raise before any test could even run.\n   **Resolved:** removed the `db.init_db()` call from `create_app()`. Schema init now happens explicitly in `app/cli.py`'s `serve` command (before `uvicorn.run`) and in `scripts/seed_demo.py`. Verified `import app.main` no longer touches disk, and the full suite plus a manual smoke test still pass.\n\n2. **`app/routes/auth.py`: the \"generate CSRF token → render template → set cookie\" sequence was duplicated verbatim in three places** (`login_form`, `_rejected`, `admin_dashboard`) — a future change to cookie attributes (e.g. `secure=True` at deploy) would need editing three call sites, and missing one would silently reintroduce an unprotected form.\n   **Resolved:** extracted a single `_render_with_csrf()` helper; all three call sites now go through it. Full suite re-run green after the refactor.\n\nFull suite: 28 passed (test-first: `tests/test_login.py` written and run red before any implementation).\nEOF\n)\""},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":263,"cache_read_input_tokens":116825,"output_tokens":746,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":161},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":263,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":746,"cache_read_input_tokens":116825,"cache_creation_input_tokens":263,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":263},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_0197EtxVJiMYvRdRLZBzmF19":{"command":"cd /Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms && gh issue comment 2 --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## /code-review findings (medium effort)\n\n1. **`app/main.py`: `db.init_db()` called unconditionally inside `create_app()`, and `app = create_app()` runs at module import time** — merely importing `app.main` wrote a real `cms.db` to the production `DATABASE_PATH` as a side effect (reproducible with `python -c \"import app.main\"`). Schema init now happens explicitly in `app/cli.py`'s `serve` command (before `uvicorn.run`) and in `scripts/seed_demo.py`. {"parentUuid":"6efc9041-0147-4aa9-a937-d9ef056ab9ab","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5","id":"msg_011CfX1uiM75UL7tWCb9JgPP","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Both review findings were already fixed, tests rerun, and the issue-2 comment posted in my previous turn. Here's the plain-English recap, then I'll push.\n\n**Two problems found by review:**\n\n1. **Importing the app wrote a database file as a side effect.** `create_app()` called `db.init_db()`, and the module ran `create_app()` automatically the moment `app.main` was imported. So even just `import app.main` — which happens at the top of the test file, before tests set up their own temp database — silently created/touched the real `cms.db` on disk. In a read-only environment (like some CI/grading sandboxes) that would crash before a single test ran.\n   **Fix:** moved the database-initialization call out of `create_app()` and into the two places that actually need it — the `cms serve` command and `scripts/seed_demo.py` — so importing the app is now side-effect-free.\n\n2. **The same three lines of CSRF code were copy-pasted three times.** Generating a CSRF token, rendering the page, and setting the cookie was repeated in the login page, the login-failure page, and the dashboard page. If someone later changed a cookie setting (like adding `secure=True` for deployment) and forgot one of the three spots, that form would silently lose CSRF protection.\n   **Fix:** pulled that logic into one shared helper (`_render_with_csrf`) that all three routes call, so there's only one place to get it right.\n\nBoth fixes were verified: `import app.main` no longer creates a database file, and the full suite (28/28) passed after each fix. The findings, fixes, and results were posted to issue #2 as a comment before committing.\n\nNow pushing the commit:"}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":407,"cache_read_input_tokens":119637,"output_tokens":618,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":407,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":618,"cache_read_input_tokens":119637,"cache_creation_input_tokens":407,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":407},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"serverClassifierRequest":"39de64c4-8624-4147-a197-99b6854a67e4","apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011CfX1uhp7SCsGCjfXDneMn","type":"assistant","uuid":"b8ef0dff-bd77-4c16-886a-26e62ef20a88","timestamp":"2026-09-29T03:25:15.792Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":null,"session_id":"a2316d11-3941-4d87-98fe-7d21dea1d9fa","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6d5bfbb1-1cff-4ca4-9c04-cac951b8d95d","version":"2.1.280","gitBranch":"main"}
